DO $$
DECLARE v_tables TEXT;
BEGIN
  SELECT string_agg(format('%I', tablename), ', ') INTO v_tables FROM pg_tables WHERE schemaname = 'public';
  IF v_tables IS NOT NULL THEN
    EXECUTE 'TRUNCATE TABLE ' || v_tables || ' RESTART IDENTITY CASCADE';
  END IF;
END $$;

-- 12. Đồng bộ lại toàn bộ PostgreSQL SERIAL/IDENTITY sequences
DO $$
DECLARE tbl TEXT; seq TEXT;
BEGIN
  FOREACH tbl IN ARRAY ARRAY['roles', 'accounts', 'customers', 'otp_tokens', 'push_subscriptions', 'farms', 'customer_contacts', 'product_types', 'suppliers', 'warehouse_categories', 'measurement_units', 'brands', 'specifications', 'product_categories', 'inventory_items', 'internal_stock_categories', 'transfer_categories', 'internal_stock_vouchers', 'internal_stock_voucher_items', 'internal_stock_voucher_numbers', 'purchase_tags', 'purchase_invoices', 'purchase_invoice_items', 'purchase_payments', 'farm_areas', 'barns', 'barn_workers', 'farming_cycles', 'flocks', 'flock_movements', 'feed_records', 'weight_samples', 'cycle_expenses', 'cycle_carried_expenses', 'farming_programs', 'farming_program_steps', 'daily_task_logs', 'work_tasks', 'work_task_checks', 'sales_invoice_numbers'] LOOP
    seq := pg_get_serial_sequence(tbl, 'id');
    IF seq IS NOT NULL THEN
      EXECUTE format('SELECT setval(%L, COALESCE((SELECT MAX(id) FROM %I), 1))', seq, tbl);
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
END $$;
